# Background sources and attribution

All background text is taken, unmodified apart from whitespace, from open-access
proceedings published by Schloss Dagstuhl - Leibniz-Zentrum fuer Informatik in the
LIPIcs series (DISC, CONCUR, OPODIS, SAND, ECRTS). The volumes are licensed under
Creative Commons Attribution licences (CC-BY 3.0 or 4.0, as stated in each file's
header); copyright in each paper remains with its authors.

Archive: `corpus-source-text-cache.tar.zst`, sha256 `a6dbdfa44dff17b7a4a2551cddb04ad12590d92fbba67b2b33e23464884c0b2c`.

2003 source files; 47 included. Duplicates (the same text in a volume
and as a separate paper or landing page) are included once.
